- `hmh07-lua:019` (original lines 1748-1760): Starts PC sticky registration and pre-sets menu chat, constructs ChatClient, binds ChatGlobal and sets default focus to ChatClient. Hidden means ChatClientShow is false; StartMenu is true when HUD is not visible, otherwise follows UI-active bit. Visibility subscriptions revalidate. It registers the close hook, runs optional PostLoadFunc and finishes sticky setup. Construction on a shared WZ HUD does not prove PS4 chat support.

- `hmh07-lua:197` (original lines 1748-1748, 1748-1760): Common lobby constructs this ZM-looking container directly; do not infer mode exclusion from its name. At construction calls HideLoadoutPreview, builds four character buttons and binds DirectorCharacterButtonZMList indices1..4. Each child setState after-hook reevaluates ZMLoadoutPreviewFrame, whose Left/LeftCenter/RightCenter/Right states follow the corresponding child's LoadoutPreviewLocal state. BotsAllowed is AlwaysFalse. MapVoteMapNext notification recreates the list. It registers IDs/close, optional postload, then calls ZombieUtility.SubscribeToLobbyClientInfo and InitOverheadNamesZM before returning self. Those calls are boundaries for this exact caller, not authorization for unrelated Zombies internals; source-ready targets are listed only. Qualified __onClose closes constructed children in source order. Actual mode membership and callback delivery remain conditional.

- `hmh07-lua:204` (original lines 1748-1748, 1748-1760): Common Hud constructs ThrustMeterContainer directly. It owns a ThrustMeter child. Hidden if HUD or weapon HUD is absent, or any listed hardcore/game-ended/moviecam/demo-hidden/killcam/flash/UI-active/vehicle/guided-missile/location-select/scoreboard/remote-streak/EMP bit is present. FullEnergy tests playerAbilities.playerEnergyRatio against1 through string-coercing model helper. Corresponding visibility/energy model subscriptions reevaluate self. CallOriginalSecond close registers self.__onClose; clips/close outside frozen span are exact dependencies. No movement or energy spending occurs. Optional postload then self return; effective HMH thrust/visibility data remains unknown.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:088` (original lines 1748-1760, 1748-1760): Builds game-status decorative panels, kills/players-left children, teammate/spectator counts and left/right endgame status lists. Nonnil aliveTeammateCount/spectatorsCount are sent directly to text. Each status display filters right-solo gameInformation versus left-solo hudGameStat, then assigns its exact endgame datasource names. Registers close, optional postload, returns self. This displays supplied data without computing ranks, population or results.

- `hmh07-lua:191` (original lines 1748-1748, 1748-1760): Fill-squad outer constructor directly creates this internal branch and changes its status/labels afterward. Builds blur/tint/focus/disabled layers, normal/focus off-status and text. Disabled = not WZUtility.IsFillSquadAvailable(controller), reevaluated on global playlistId or lobbyList.playerCount changes. This child does not install a toggle button or write the party-fill setting. Qualified reset and DefaultState/Disabled focus clips control brightness/brackets/status text, register transition/interrupted handlers and loop nextClip=Focus where specified. Linked __onClose closes FocusBrackets then DotTiledBacking; CallOriginalSecond close hook, optional postload then self return. - `hmh07-lua:179` (original lines 1748-1748, 1748-1760): Common lobby creates MapVotePC directly, alongside a separate MapVoteZM; a PC-looking field name is not a construction gate. Builds decided/random/previous/next entries. Model subscriptions update map images, names, mode names/icons and counts only for nonnil values; lobbyStatus text is uppercased. Confirm on random/next calls LobbyMapVoteSelectRandom/Next and returns true. Previous confirm and label require MapVotePreviousSelectable; disallowed confirm falls through without success and label returns false. Chosen child events play the corresponding parent clip then dispatch to children; focus handlers try local or super gainFocus, refresh confirm label and return its result. States are VOTING->MapVote, LOCKEDIN plus exact vote enum->ChosenNext/Previous/Random, and LOCKEDIN->SelectedMap. MapVote/lobbyNav/voteType notifications only schedule state reevaluation, not choose a vote. The setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally arranges SelectedMap on clip-over. Constructor returns self, close uses self.__onClose through CallOriginalSecond. Frozen class clip/reset/close companions are source-linked, with per-state transactions retained below; actual enum priority, backend vote delivery, callbacks, animation completion/cancellation and effective HMH visibility remain external.

- `hmh07-lua-follow3:010` (original lines 1748-1760): Creates the selection instruction, specialist image and specialist name. Both character-dependent children subscribe to CharacterSelection.characterIndex and update only for nonnil model values. It registers close, conditionally calls PostLoadFunc and returns self. Character display lookup/rendering and the actual selection action remain external. Constructs CoD.NewSpecialistFooter from the displayed menu/controller/layout arguments. Creates SelForNewSpawn as LUI.UIText.new, SpecialistImage as LUI.UIFixedAspectRatioImage.new, SpecialistName as LUI.UIText.new. Connects 2 model/path references; the model subscription effects below specify which receiver changes and under which nil/value guard. Installs 1 event/action/override bindings; each callback keeps its captured controller/menu/widget references and executes only on its bound delivery. - `hmh07-lua:228` (original lines 1748-1748, 1748-1760): Cited WZ safe-area caller directly constructs this ammo/equipment container. Creates AmmoWidgetWZ, ability-item(unit189) bound to LethalOffhands.0, equipment and attachment quick-access widgets. Equipment filters include only type equipment, equipped0, quickEquip1; attachment filters require type attachment, availableAction!=NONE, notAvailable0, quickEquip1. Both small lists have10 columns and both sizes use WarzoneInventory. Equipment current/mid/top bind WarzoneInventoryGlobal equipment/equipment0/equipment1. Attachment InventoryOpen defaults false, InventoryOpenRightAligned follows quick inventory open, InventoryClosedRightAligned defaults true; inventory/settings/selection/hint/free-slot/input changes reevaluate. list_item_gain_focus sends SendInventoryItemFocusNotify and returns nil (does not claim event consumed). Self HiddenPreference uses weapon/equipment UI preference; Visible requires playerSpawned, not caster, appropriate positive HUD/weapon visibility and absence of demo/hardcore/remote/opaque/flash/scoped/scoreboard/UI/vehicle-hide/infiltration/PC-inventory exclusions. VisibleCodCaster requires caster+inventory profile1, not ended/vehicle-hide/infiltration. VisibleKillcam requires noncaster, no vehicle-hide/infiltration/PC-inventory and killcam bit. Exact guards in trace. State/input/model notifications reevaluate only. Close is registered via CallOriginalSecond to unselected __onClose; optional postload then AddQuickAccessInventoryWidget for equipment slot4 true and attachments slot5 false; return self. No item debit/equip/network operation is proved by these display/filter registrations; source data, settings and callback/lifetime/native gaps remain.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:005` (original lines 1777-1779, 1777-1779, 1748-1760, 1748-1760): The constructor requests persistent seenWZInGameAAR initialization only when its child handle is falsey, and prepares merit reward models with false, then requests WZAAR_Containers and an empty focus target. Visible requires the aarVisible model value to be literal true; the registered model subscription requests reevaluation only when delivered. The registered setState callback, when delivered and its Visible predicate succeeds, requests LockInputHUD(self,true), MenuHidesFreeCursor(menu,delivered controller), then SetupMeritModels(true). MenuHidesFreeCursor does work only when usingfreecursor is truthy: it handles a prior delayed timer, may register close/unhide, and if hidFreeCursor is falsey sets that flag and either clears truthy ignoreNextMenuHides or requests hidden:get()+1. Actual cursor/native result is unknown. The DefaultState branch requests LockInputHUD(false) and lose focus. It registers close, may request PostLoadFunc, and returns only if reached. No independent close/unlock-on-error guarantee, callback delivery/order, persistent seen/AAR/backend provenance or runtime behavior is established.

- `hmh07-lua:248` (original lines 1748-1748, 1748-1760): MapVote directly constructs decided/random/previous/next entries. Selected and Unselectable defaults are AlwaysFalse; MapVote parent merges Selected=true on decided entry and Previous-selectability condition on previous. Thus exact caller override matters; own default false does not exclude those states globally. Qualified reset/state/focus clips change selection/disable visuals and exact timing; __onClose closes layout/backing/button-outline through CallOriginalSecond. No confirm/network vote call here; parent unit179 owns it. Optional postload then self return.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:028` (original lines 1748-1760): Builds a spawn-beacon radius/icon/label/pulse/name. Name states map exact gamemodeFlags 0..4 to threat/EMP/manual labels plus the inactive-beacon predicate. Parent Destroyed uses the disabled-or-destroyed helper and Hidden calls AlwaysFalse. It does not activate or spawn a beacon. Creates self via LUI.UIElement.new, SpawnSelectRadius via CoD.SpawnRegionRadius.new, SpawnSelectIconBg via CoD.SpawnSelectLocationMarker.new, SpawnSelectLabel via CoD.SpawnSelectionLabel.new, SpawnSelectPulse via CoD.SpawnRegionPulse.new, SpawnBeaconPoint via CoD.SpawnRegionName.new. Stores state predicates Destroyed, ThreatLow, ThreatMedium, ThreatHigh, DisabledEMP, ManualDisabled, Destroyed, Hidden; their body effects require state-system evaluation.

This constructor builds an initially hidden image. CharacterRestricted and OneAbilityVisible predicates are AlwaysFalse; Visible requires neither ShowDirectorPregame nor IsPublicWZLobby, then exact self isRestricted true. lobbyNav and isRestricted notifications reevaluate state. Hence its OWN Visible path is excluded when the cited IsPublicWZLobby predicate is true; that is a scoped source condition, not an exclusion of the whole constructor or caller overrides. Reset hides the image; linked visible companions show/change it. No close override is registered in this primary span. Optional PostLoadFunc then self return.

Own Visible requires draw_spectator_messages and spectating_client with final killcam/in-killcam/opaque hash/play-of-match/scoreboard/team-spectator/ui-active bits absent. - `hmh07-lua-follow1:011` (original lines 1748-1760, 1748-1760): Creates an AbilityCallout_Queue UIElement and subscribes PerController.scriptNotify. Queue filtering/dispatch/teardown belongs to the external helper; no close/reset body is selected here.
